# SRS V1.4 鈫?PIS V2 Functional Coverage Matrix

Baseline SHA: `3a7f44b69408d31794aeb9a605690134e21a18a0`

Authoritative requirements: [SRS-V14-FUNCTIONAL-BASELINE.md](./SRS-V14-FUNCTIONAL-BASELINE.md)

## 1. Audit method and current coverage

This is a code-evidence audit of the real baseline SHA. A requirement is **COMPLETE** only when the repository contains a coherent implementation across the necessary domain/application/API/DB/UI/test surfaces. **PARTIAL** means a concrete subset exists but closure is missing. **MISSING** means no production implementation evidence was found. **EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY** never means production verified.

| Metric | Count |
|---|---|---:|
| Total requirements | 750 |
| COMPLETE | 280 |
| PARTIAL | 168 |
| MISSING | 224 |
| EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY | 78 |
| CONFLICT_RESOLVED_BY_V2_MODEL | 0 |
| Current coverage (COMPLETE / total) | 37.3% |

### 1.1 Scope classification

Every requirement row has a `Scope` column. The classification is deliberately
separate from implementation status:

| Scope | Meaning |
|---|---|
| BUSINESS | Directly operated, maintained, or consumed by pathology staff, department managers, or hospital business users. |
| EXTERNAL_PLATFORM | HIS/LIS/PACS/EMR, provincial/city platform, or other external business-system boundary. |
| DATA_PLATFORM | Data collection and reporting front-service infrastructure. |
| AI | AI provider/model integration and clinical model validation. |
| SECURITY_INFRA | CA/HSM/national-cryptography, security infrastructure, and final compliance operations. |
| NFR | Non-functional targets and final performance/DR verification. |

The business completion target uses only rows with `Scope = BUSINESS`. An
`EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY` row remains a product requirement: it is complete only
after the internal Port, Adapter contract, Simulator, configuration, UI,
audit, and failure handling are present. A real external environment is not
claimed without production evidence.

## 2. Evidence registry

- **E-REG**: `V11/V12`, `V2RegistrationController`, `V2RegistrationWorkbench.vue`, registration domain/web/Postgres tests.
- **E-CASE**: `V2CaseWorkspaceController`, `CaseProgressProjectionApplicationService`, `V2CaseContext.vue`, Gate C and PX01鈥揚X03 E2E.
- **E-MAT**: `V13`, material domain/application/controllers, grossing/production/custody UI and tests.
- **E-PROD**: `V26/V27`, histology facts/controllers, production workbench and focused-workspace E2E.
- **E-TECH**: `V15`, TechnicalOrder domain/application/controller/UI and web/E2E tests.
- **E-CYTO**: BusinessType capability, direct specimen-to-slide production and cytology E2E.
- **E-FROZEN**: `V17`, frozen module/workspace, diagnosis/report integration and tests.
- **E-DX**: `V14`, diagnosis domain/application/controller, Viewer-first UI and tests.
- **E-WSI**: `V19`, DigitalSlide module, OpenSeadragon adapter and WSI/no-WSI tests.
- **E-RPT**: `V16`, Report domain/application/PDF renderer/Report Center and tests.
- **E-WB**: permission/data-fact Workbench projections and PX03C-R1 tests.
- **E-MOL**: `V18` molecular result baseline and molecular API.
- **E-SEC**: runtime auth, P15 authorization, administration, `V20/V24`, security tests.
- **E-CUST**: `V19` archive/loan/destruction tables, custody API and UI.
- **E-SEND**: consultation/send-out modules and custody relations.
- **E-QC**: `V19` QC rules/evaluations, QC API/UI.
- **E-DEVICE**: `V21/V23` device configuration and DeviceAdapter port tests.
- **E-STATS**: statistics summary API/repository/UI.
- **E-CONF**: configuration APIs, site profiles, BusinessType/number/template/project configuration.
- **E-INT**: `V22鈥揤24` message log, retry, dead letter, replay, reconciliation, identifier and identity integration foundation.
- **E-ID**: external identity provider/link/audit foundation; no production CA verification.
- **E-MIG**: `V25` migration runs, manifests, staging, exceptions, checkpoints and validation reports.
- **E-CONS**: consultation external-material baseline; regional sharing remains open.
- **E-NFR**: modular monolith, browser client, configuration and existing quality gates.
- **E-NONE**: no repository implementation evidence found.

## 4. Conflict resolutions mandated by V2

- Case operational stages remain projections; Case lifecycle is not expanded.
- Technical phase records remain optional facts and are not a mandatory MES workflow.
- Block and Slide stay unified across routine, frozen, cytology, technical-order and external sources.
- INITIAL/REVIEW/AUDIT remain one Diagnosis signing/review process.
- Every sign-out creates one immutable Report; no ReportVersion wrapper.
- Workbench items remain projections from permission, scope and facts; no persisted generic WorkItem.
- Frozen/Routine provenance uses an explicit source relation, not Generic CaseRelation.
- External fee/CA/device/platform/AI failures cannot corrupt or lock the core pathology transaction.

## 5. Top 20 missing capability clusters

The following list is prioritized by patient safety, clinical-flow closure and dependency order. Every referenced ID is currently `MISSING`; adjacent `PARTIAL` requirements are handled in the same wave.

1. **E06鈥揈10** 鈥?gross images, imaging-station boundary, Case binding, annotation and measurement.
2. **M15鈥揗17** 鈥?Viewer annotation, measurement and screenshot evidence.
3. **L13, L28鈥揕29** 鈥?maximum diagnosis workload, favorites and follow-up.
4. **N09, N13, N24** 鈥?common tumor templates, PDF encryption and self-service printing.
5. **O12** 鈥?user notification center driven by business facts.
6. **Q09鈥換12, Q18** 鈥?molecular instrument/reagent bindings and molecular Workbench queue.
7. **R06, R16** 鈥?password management and staff scheduling.
8. **T06鈥揟12** 鈥?courier, tracking, logistics exceptions and common addresses.
9. **U01鈥揢13** 鈥?critical-value definition, notification, acknowledgement, history, query, export and TAT.
10. **W01鈥揥12** 鈥?controlled quality-system documents and revisions.
11. **X08鈥揦18** 鈥?equipment program, usage, maintenance, repair, lifecycle, warning and statistics.
12. **Y01鈥揧20** 鈥?consumable/reagent catalog, inventory, batch, expiry, consumption, quality and audit.
13. **Z01鈥揨12** 鈥?procurement request, approval, contract, receipt, archive and financial reference.
14. **AA01鈥揂A17** 鈥?department-space hierarchy, zones, environment, safety, alarms and history.
15. **AB06, AB25鈥揂B27** 鈥?income analytics, Excel/PDF export and configurable reports.
16. **AG01鈥揂G36** 鈥?Data Collection Front Service end-to-end.
17. **AH09鈥揂H12, AH18鈥揂H20** 鈥?WSI storage tier, archive index/search, integrity, archive and restore.
18. **AI01鈥揂I14** 鈥?AI provider port, simulator-ready result schema, history and Viewer overlay.
19. **AJ05鈥揂J14** 鈥?regional case/WSI sharing, authorization expiry, access logs and statistics.
20. **AL09, AL18** 鈥?export control and security-event management.

## 6. Recommended wave sequence

1. **Wave 1 鈥?Core clinical closure:** registration/receiving corrections, specimen/grossing gaps, production rework, diagnosis queue gaps, Viewer tools, report re-sign/distribution boundaries.
2. **Wave 2 鈥?Archive, loan, critical value, logistics:** physical inventory/inspection, approvals, package/courier tracking, critical-value acknowledgement and communication history.
3. **Wave 3 鈥?QC, statistics, templates, configuration:** quality dimensions, periods/exports/warnings, BI drill-down, tumor templates, TAT and dictionaries.
4. **Wave 4 鈥?Equipment, consumables, procurement, department space, quality documents.**
5. **Wave 5 鈥?Molecular, regional collaboration, report distribution.**
6. **Wave 6 鈥?HIS/LIS/EMR/PACS/device/CA adapters:** adapter + simulator + observability; no production claim without real environment.
7. **Wave 7 鈥?Data Collection Front Service.**
8. **Wave 8 鈥?Digital Slide Archive and scanner-format adapters.**
9. **Wave 9 鈥?AI provider framework, simulator, result schema, UI and Viewer overlay.**
10. **Wave 10 鈥?Security, migration, performance, backup/recovery and system-wide closure.**

Each wave starts with a fresh origin/main sync, uses an independent commit, runs relevant backend/frontend/E2E/browser acceptance, pushes without force, and stops on semantic conflict.

## 7. Coverage interpretation

The target is not reached at this baseline. Current coverage is 37.3%; 168 partial and 224 missing requirements require implementation. External dependencies remain explicitly separated from implementation readiness and production verification.
